  function injectSafeGuide(){
    const content = document.querySelector('#screen-profile .content');
    if (!content || document.getElementById('cb75-safe-guide')) return;
    const samuBtn = content.querySelector('.btn-samu-trigger');
    if (samuBtn) samuBtn.insertAdjacentHTML('afterend', guideHtml());
    else content.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', guideHtml());
  }

<script>
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  CUIDARBEM — FEATURE PACK v2
//  1. Streak counter  2. Haptic  3. Swipe  4. Voice  5. Kiosk  6. Mode
//  7. BP/Glucose  8. PDF export  9. Auto backup  10. Onboarding
//  11. Alarm sound  12. Confirm med with camera photo
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// ── 1. STREAK COUNTER ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function calcStreak() {
  let streak = 0;
  const allTasks = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('cuidarbem_tasks') || '[]');
  const comp = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('cuidarbem_completions') || '{}');
  for (let i = 0; i < 365; i++) {
    const d = new Date(); d.setDate(d.getDate() - i);
    const ds = localDateKey(d);
    const dayTasks = allTasks.filter(t => t.date === ds || t.repeat === 'daily');
    if (!dayTasks.length) { if (i > 0) break; continue; }
    const allDone = dayTasks.every(t => comp[`${t.id}_${ds}`]);
    if (allDone) streak++;
    else if (i > 0) break; // today incomplete is ok, yesterday must be complete
  }
  return streak;
}

function updateStreakBadge() {
  const streak = calcStreak();
  const badge = document.getElementById('streak-badge');
  const count = document.getElementById('streak-count');
  if (badge && count) {
    count.textContent = streak;
    badge.style.display = streak >= 2 ? 'block' : 'none';
  }
}

// ── 2. HAPTIC FEEDBACK ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function haptic(pattern = [30]) {
  if ('vibrate' in navigator) navigator.vibrate(pattern);
}

// ── 3. SWIPE BETWEEN SCREENS ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
(function setupSwipe() {
  const screens = ['home','calendar','dashboard','ocr','reports','appt','profile'];
  let startX = 0, startY = 0;

  document.addEventListener('touchstart', e => {
    startX = e.touches[0].clientX;
    startY = e.touches[0].clientY;
  }, { passive: true });

  document.addEventListener('touchend', e => {
    const dx = e.changedTouches[0].clientX - startX;
    const dy = e.changedTouches[0].clientY - startY;
    if (Math.abs(dx) < 60 || Math.abs(dy) > Math.abs(dx) * 0.7) return; // not horizontal enough
    if (document.getElementById('kiosk-overlay').style.display === 'flex') return;

    const active = document.querySelector('.screen.active');
    if (!active) return;
    const activeId = active.id.replace('screen-', '');
    const idx = screens.indexOf(activeId);
    if (idx === -1) return;

    let next = -1;
    i
ue || 'daily';
    task.obs = (byId('edit-med-obs')?.value || '').trim();
    task.prescriptionExpiry = (byId('edit-med-rx')?.value || '').trim();
    task.nextPickup = (byId('edit-med-pickup')?.value || '').trim();
    task.updatedAt = Date.now();
    saveTasks();
    closeEditMedModal();
    renderAll();
    buildWeekStrip();
    if (typeof scheduleAlarmsOnSW === 'function') scheduleAlarmsOnSW();
    if (typeof window.cbSupabaseQueuePush === 'function') window.cbSupabaseQueuePush('edit-medication');
    setTimeout(() => openMedInfo(task.id), 50);
    showToast('✅ Medicamento atualizado.');
  };
})();
</script>

<style id="cuidarbem-v48-edit-med-stable">
  .edit-med-overlay{backdrop-filter:blur(8px);overscroll-behavior:contain;}
  .edit-med-overlay.open{animation:none!important;}
  .edit-med-card{transform:none!important;animation:none!important;will-change:auto!important;}
  body.cb-edit-med-open{overflow:hidden!important;touch-action:none;}
  body.cb-edit-med-open .med-info-overlay{opacity:0!important;pointer-events:none!important;}

<script id="cuidarbem-v48-edit-med-stable-js">
(function(){
  function byId(id){ return document.getElementById(id); }
  function getCurrentMedTask(){
    const list = (typeof tasks !== 'undefined' ? tasks : []);
    return list.find(t => t.id === window.currentMedInfoTaskId || t.id === currentMedInfoTaskId);
  }
  window.editCurrentMedFromInfo = function(){
    const task = getCurrentMedTask();
    if (!task) { showToast('Não encontrei este medicamento para editar.'); return; }

    // Fecha visualmente a janela de detalhes antes de abrir o editor.
    // Isso evita duas camadas/modais animando ao mesmo tempo no mobile.
    const info = byId('med-info-overlay');
    if (info) info.classList.remove('open');

    byId('edit-med-name').value = task.name || '';
    byId('edit-med-dose').value = task.dose || '';
    byId('edit-med-time').value = task.time || '';
    byId('edit-med-date').value = task.date || todayStr();
    byId('edit-med-repeat').value = task.repeat || 'daily';
    byId('edit-med-obs').value = task.obs || '';
    byId('edit-med-rx').value = task.prescriptionExpiry || '';
    byId('edit-med-pickup').value = task.nextPickup || '';

    document.body.classList.add('cb-edit-med-open');
    const overlay = byId('edit-med-overlay');
    if (overlay) overlay.classList.add('open');
  };

  window.closeEditMedModal = function(){
    const overlay = byId('edit-med-overlay');
    if (overlay) overlay.classList.remove('open');
    document.body.classList.remove('cb-edit-med-open');
  };

  window.saveEditMedModal = function(){
    const task = getCurrentMedTask();
    if (!task) { showToast('Não encontrei este medicamento para salvar.'); return; }
    const name = (byId('edit-med-name')?.value || '').trim();
    if (!name) { showToast('Informe o nome do medicamento.'); return; }

    task.name = name;
    task.dose = (byId('edit-med-dose')?.value || '').trim();
    task.time = (byId('edit-med-time')?.value || '').trim();
    task.date = (byId('edit-med-date')?.value || '').trim() || todayStr();
    task.repeat = byId('edit-med-repeat')?.value || 'daily';
    task.obs = (byId('edit-med-obs')?.value || '').trim();
    task.prescriptionExpiry = (byId('edit-med-rx')?.value || '').trim();
    task.next
;
      edits.forEach((btn, idx) => { if (idx > 0) btn.remove(); });
    });
  });
})();
</script>

<style id="cuidarbem-v48-edit-med-stable">
  .edit-med-overlay{backdrop-filter:blur(8px);overscroll-behavior:contain;}
  .edit-med-overlay.open{animation:none!important;}
  .edit-med-card{transform:none!important;animation:none!important;will-change:auto!important;}
  body.cb-edit-med-open{overflow:hidden!important;touch-action:none;}
  body.cb-edit-med-open .med-info-overlay{opacity:0!important;pointer-events:none!important;}

<script id="cuidarbem-v48-edit-med-stable-js">
(function(){
  function byId(id){ return document.getElementById(id); }
  function getCurrentMedTask(){
    const list = (typeof tasks !== 'undefined' ? tasks : []);
    return list.find(t => t.id === window.currentMedInfoTaskId || t.id === currentMedInfoTaskId);
  }
  window.editCurrentMedFromInfo = function(){
    const task = getCurrentMedTask();
    if (!task) { showToast('Não encontrei este medicamento para editar.'); return; }

    // Fecha visualmente a janela de detalhes antes de abrir o editor.
    // Isso evita duas camadas/modais animando ao mesmo tempo no mobile.
    const info = byId('med-info-overlay');
    if (info) info.classList.remove('open');

    byId('edit-med-name').value = task.name || '';
    byId('edit-med-dose').value = task.dose || '';
    byId('edit-med-time').value = task.time || '';
    byId('edit-med-date').value = task.date || todayStr();
    byId('edit-med-repeat').value = task.repeat || 'daily';
    byId('edit-med-obs').value = task.obs || '';
    byId('edit-med-rx').value = task.prescriptionExpiry || '';
    byId('edit-med-pickup').value = task.nextPickup || '';

    document.body.classList.add('cb-edit-med-open');
    const overlay = byId('edit-med-overlay');
    if (overlay) overlay.classList.add('open');
  };

  window.closeEditMedModal = function(){
    const overlay = byId('edit-med-overlay');
    if (overlay) overlay.classList.remove('open');
    document.body.classList.remove('cb-edit-med-open');
  };

  window.saveEditMedModal = function(){
    const task = getCurrentMedTask();
    if (!task) { showToast('Não encontrei este medicamento para salvar.'); return; }
    const name = (byId('edit-med-name')?.value || '').trim();
    if (!name) { showToast('Informe o nome do medicamento.'); return; }

    task.name = name;
    task.dose = (byId('edit-med-dose')?.value || '').trim();
    task.time = (byId('edit-med-time')?.value || '').trim();
    task.date = (byId('edit-med-date')?.value || '').trim() || todayStr();
    task.repeat = byId('edit-med-repeat')?.value || 'daily';
    task.obs = (byId('edit-med-obs')?.value || '').trim();
    task.prescriptionExpiry = (byId('edit-med-rx')?.value || '').trim();
    task.nextPickup = (byId('edit-med-pickup')?.value || '').trim();
    task.updatedAt = Date.now();

    saveTasks();
    closeEditMedModal();
    renderAll();
    buildWeekStrip();

tentes.

<script id="cuidarbem-v70-stability-script">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const rerender = () => {
    try{
      if (typeof renderReports === 'function' && document.getElementById('screen-reports')?.classList.contains('active')) {
        setTimeout(renderReports, 80);
      }
      if (typeof rerenderReports === 'function' && document.getElementById('screen-reports')?.classList.contains('active')) {
        setTimeout(rerenderReports, 120);
      }
    }catch(e){ console.warn('[CuidarBem v70] estabilidade visual:', e); }
  };
  window.addEventListener('resize', rerender, { passive:true });
  window.addEventListener('orientationchange', () => setTimeout(rerender, 220), { passive:true });
  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => { if(!document.hidden) setTimeout(rerender, 160); });
})();
</script>

<!-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
     LIMPEZA PERMANENTE DE EFEITOS — confirmado por diagnóstico
     Remove backdrop-filter, animações de layout e will-change
     que causavam flashes e falhas no mobile
     ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ -->
<style id="cb-effects-cleanup">

/* ── 1. backdrop-filter removido globalmente ─────────────────────
   40 ocorrências espalhadas causavam repaint de GPU em todo scroll.
   Overlays de modal mantêm um overlay escuro simples via background.  */
* { backdrop-filter: none !important; -webkit-backdrop-filter: none !important; }

/* ── 2. Animações em elementos de layout (screen, header, nav) ────
   cbDesktopFadeIn e cbScreenIn sem @media disparavam no mobile
   causando opacity:0→1 + translateY em cada troca de guia. */
.screen,
.screen.active,
.header,
.header-sub,
.bottom-nav,
.content {
  animation: none !important;
  transition: none !important;
}

/* ── 3. will-change removido de todos (criava camadas GPU extras) */
* { will-change: auto !important; }

/* ── 4. isolation:isolate em containers de layout (interfere sticky) */
.screen,
.screen.active,
.header,
.header-sub,
.content {
  isolation: auto !important;
}

/* ── 5. Glass backgrounds → opacos (blur foi removido, translúcido
   sem blur fica sem sentido e mais lento) */
.bottom-nav {
  background: var(--white) !important;
  /* dark mode */
}
.bottom-nav.dark-mode-element,
body.dark .bottom-nav {
  background: var(--card-bg) !important;
}

/* ── 6. Overlays ficam com fundo opaco — sem blur */
.confirm-overlay,
.med-info-overlay,
.appt-confirm-overlay,
.modal-overlay,
.cb-delete-overlay,
.edit-task-overlay,
.edit-med-overlay,
.photo-modal-overlay,
.cb75-modal-overlay,
.result-viewer-overlay,
.alerts-center-overlay,
.samu-overlay {
  background: rgba(0,0,0,0.55) !important;
}

/* ── 7. Transitions simples MANTIDAS só em interações de botão ────
   (não afetam layout, não causam reflow) */
.nav-btn,
.btn,
.btn-primary,
.btn-secondary,
.btn-action-small,
.task-item,
.med-pill {
  transition: opacity 0.12s, transform 0.12s !important;
}

/* ── 8. Corrige sidebar desktop descentralizado ──────────────────
   O killswitch do diagnóstico apagava o transform da sidebar,
   deixando ela colada em left:0 sem compensação.
   Garante que no desktop o sidebar fica fixo à esquerda (correto). */
@media (min-width: 768px) {
  .bottom-nav {
    position: fixed !important;
    top: 0 !important;
    left: 0 !important;
    bottom: 0 !important;
    transform: none !important;
    width: var(--sidebar-w, 260px) !important;
    height: 100svh !important;
    max-width: none !important;
  }
}

/* ── 9. Botão e painel de diagnóstico — centrado na área de conteúdo */
#cb-diag-btn {
  position: fixed;
  bo
width: 767px) {
  .screen:not(.active) {

<!-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
     LIMPEZA PERMANENTE DE EFEITOS — confirmado por diagnóstico
     Remove backdrop-filter, animações de layout e will-change
     que causavam flashes e falhas no mobile
     ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ -->
<style id="cb-effects-cleanup">

/* ── 1. backdrop-filter removido globalmente ─────────────────────
   40 ocorrências espalhadas causavam repaint de GPU em todo scroll.
   Overlays de modal mantêm um overlay escuro simples via background.  */
* { backdrop-filter: none !important; -webkit-backdrop-filter: none !important; }

/* ── 2. Animações em elementos de layout (screen, header, nav) ────
   cbDesktopFadeIn e cbScreenIn sem @media disparavam no mobile
   causando opacity:0→1 + translateY em cada troca de guia. */
.screen,
.screen.active,
.header,
.header-sub,
.bottom-nav,
.content {
  animation: none !important;
  transition: none !important;
}

/* ── 3. will-change removido de todos (criava camadas GPU extras) */
* { will-change: auto !important; }

/* ── 4. isolation:isolate em containers de layout (interfere sticky) */
.screen,
.screen.active,
.header,
.header-sub,
.content {
  isolation: auto !important;
}

/* ── 5. Glass backgrounds → opacos (blur foi removido, translúcido
   sem blur fica sem sentido e mais lento) */
.bottom-nav {
  background: var(--white) !important;
  /* dark mode */
}
.bottom-nav.dark-mode-element,
body.dark .bottom-nav {
  background: var(--card-bg) !important;
}

/* ── 6. Overlays ficam com fundo opaco — sem blur */
.confirm-overlay,
.med-info-overlay,
.appt-confirm-overlay,
.modal-overlay,
.cb-delete-overlay,
.edit-task-overlay,
.edit-med-overlay,
.photo-modal-overlay,
.cb75-modal-overlay,
.result-viewer-overlay,
.alerts-center-overlay,
.samu-overlay {
  background: rgba(0,0,0,0.55) !important;
}

/* ── 7. Transitions simples MANTIDAS só em interações de botão ────
   (não afetam layout, não causam reflow) */
.nav-btn,
.btn,
.btn-primary,
.btn-secondary,
.btn-action-small,
.task-item,
.med-pill {
  transition: opacity 0.12s, transform 0.12s !important;
}

/* ── 8. Corrige sidebar desktop descentralizado ──────────────────
   O killswitch do diagnóstico apagava o transform da sidebar,
   deixando ela colada em left:0 sem compensação.
   Garante que no desktop o sidebar fica fixo à esquerda (correto). */
@media (min-width: 768px) {
  .bottom-nav {
    position: fixed !important;
    top: 0 !important;
    left: 0 !important;
    bottom: 0 !important;
    transform: none !important;
    width: var(--sidebar-w, 260px) !important;
    height: 100svh !important;
    max-width: none !important;
  }
}

/* ── 9. Botão e painel de diagnóstico — centrado na área de conteúdo */
#cb-diag-btn {
  position: fixed;
  bottom: calc(var(--nav-h, 72px) + 12px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom));
  right: 14px;
  /* Desktop: afasta do sidebar */
  z-index: 99999;
  background: #1a1a1a;
  color: #fff;
  border: none;
  border-radius: 999px;
  padding: 8px 14px;
  font-size: 11px;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: .04em;
  cursor: pointer;
  box-shadow: 0 4px 16px rgba(0,0,0,.4);
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 6px;
  opacity: .75;
  -webkit-tap-highlight-color: transparent;
  user-select: none;
}
#cb-diag-btn:active { opacity: 1; }
#cb-diag-btn .cb-diag-dot {
  width: 8px; height: 8px; border-radius: 50%;
  background: #4ade80; flex-shrink: 0;
}

/* Painel centralizado NA ÁREA DE CONTEÚDO (desktop: após sidebar) */
#cb-diag-panel {
  position: fixed;
  top: 50%;
  /* mobile: centrado no viewpo
tant;
    visibility: hidden !important;
    height: 0 !important;
    overflow: hidden !important;
    pointer-events: none !important;
  }
}

</style>

<script id="cb-wheel-router">
/* Desktop: rota wheel para o container scrollável sob o cursor.
   Home screen: grid com overflow:hidden — detecta painel por posição X do cursor. */
(function(){
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;

  function getScrollParent(el) {
    while (el && el !== document.documentElement) {
      var st = window.getComputedStyle(el);
      var oy = st.overflowY;
      if ((oy === 'auto' || oy === 'scroll') && el.scrollHeight > el.clientHeight) {
        return el;
      }
      el = el.parentElement;
    }
    return null;
  }

  document.addEventListener('wheel', function(e) {
    if (window.innerWidth < 768) return;

    // Tela Início: grid com overflow:hidden — getScrollParent falha.
    // Roteia pelo painel sob o cursor (esquerda=content, direita=right-panel).
    var home = document.getElementById('screen-home');
    if (home && home.classList.contains('active')) {
      var rightPanel = home.querySelector('.desktop-right-panel');
      var panel = (rightPanel && e.clientX >= rightPanel.getBoundingClientRect().left)
        ? rightPanel : home.querySelector('.content');
      if (panel && panel.scrollHeight > panel.clientHeight) {
        e.preventDefault();
        panel.scrollBy({ top: e.deltaY * (e.deltaMode === 1 ? 20 : 1), behavior: 'auto' });
      }
      return; // sempre consome o evento na home
    }

    // Outras telas: sobe a árvore até o scroll container natural
    var target = getScrollParent(e.target);
    if (!target) return;

    var atTop    = target.scrollTop <= 0;
    var atBottom = target.scrollTop + target.clientHeight >= target.scrollHeight - 1;
    if ((e.deltaY < 0 && atTop) || (e.deltaY > 0 && atBottom)) return;

    e.preventDefault();
    target.scrollBy({ top: e.deltaY * (e.deltaMode === 1 ? 20 : 1), behavior: 'auto' });
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

<script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',function(){
  document.querySelectorAll('.nav-btn').forEach(btn=>{
    btn.addEventListener('click',function(){
      document.querySelectorAll('.nav-btn').forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('active'));
      this.classList.add('active');

      const target = this.getAttribute('data-screen');
      if(!target) return;

      document.querySelectorAll('.screen').forEach(s=>s.classList.remove('active'));
      const el = document.getElementById(target);
      if(el) el.classList.add('active');
    });
  });
});
</script>

.btn-action-small {
  flex: 1; padding: 10px 8px; border: 1.5px solid var(--gray-200);

  font-family:
ay');
  const title = document.getElementById('cb-delete-title');
  const msg = document.getElementById('cb-delete-message');
  const btn = document.getElementById('cb-delete-confirm-btn');
  if (!overlay || !title || !msg || !btn) {
    if (cbDeleteConfirmCallback) cbDeleteConfirmCallback();
    return;
  }
  title.textContent = o.title || 'Excluir?';
  msg.textContent = o.message || 'Esta ação não pode ser desfeita.';
  btn.textContent = o.confirmText || 'Excluir';
  btn.onclick = function(){
    const fn = cbDeleteConfirmCallback;
    cbDeleteConfirmCallback = null;
    overlay.classList.remove('open');
    if (fn) fn();
  };
  overlay.classList.add('open');
  setTimeout(()=>btn.focus && btn.focus(), 80);
}
function closeDeleteConfirm(ev){
  if (ev && ev.target && ev.target.id !== 'cb-delete-overlay') return;
  cbDeleteConfirmCallback = null;
  const overlay = document.getElementById('cb-delete-overlay');
  if (overlay) overlay.classList.remove('open');
}
document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){
  if (e.key === 'Escape') closeDeleteConfirm();
});

letions') || '{}');
  for (let i = 0; i < 365; i++) {
    const d = new Date(); d.setDate(d.getDate() - i);
    const ds = localDateKey(d);
    const dayTasks = allTasks.filter(t => t.date === ds || t.repeat === 'daily');
    if (!dayTasks.length) { if (i > 0) break; continue; }
    const allDone = dayTasks.every(t => comp[`${t.id}_${ds}`]);
    if (allDone) streak++;
    else if (i > 0) break; // today incomplete is ok, yesterday must be complete
  }
  return streak;
}

function updateStreakBadge() {
  const streak = calcStreak();
  const badge = document.getElementById('streak-badge');
  const count = document.getElementById('streak-count');
  if (badge && count) {
    count.textContent = streak;
    badge.style.display = streak >= 2 ? 'block' : 'none';
  }
}

// ── 2. HAPTIC FEEDBACK ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function haptic(pattern = [30]) {
  if ('vibrate' in navigator) navigator.vibrate(pattern);
}

// ── 3. SWIPE BETWEEN SCREENS ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
(function setupSwipe() {
  const screens = ['home','calendar','dashboard','ocr','reports','appt','profile'];
  let startX = 0, startY = 0;

  document.addEventListener('touchstart', e => {
    startX = e.touches[0].clientX;
    startY = e.touches[0].clientY;
  }, { passive: true });

  document.addEventListener('touchend', e => {
    const dx = e.changedTouches[0].clientX - startX;
    const dy = e.changedTouches[0].clientY - startY;
    if (Math.abs(dx) < 60 || Math.abs(dy) > Math.abs(dx) * 0.7) return; // not horizontal enough
    if (document.getElementById('kiosk-overlay').style.display === 'flex') return;

    const active = document.querySelector('.screen.active');
    if (!active) return;
    const activeId = active.id.replace('screen-', '');
    const idx = screens.indexOf(activeId);
    if (idx === -1) return;

    let next = -1;
    if (dx < -60 && idx < screens.length - 1) next = idx + 1;
    if (dx > 60 && idx > 0) next = idx - 1;
    if (next === -1) return;

    const nextName = screens[next];
    const btn = document.getElementById(`nav-${nextName}`);
    if (btn) { haptic([15]); goScreen(nextName, btn); }
  }, { passive: true });
})();





// ── v58. INTELIGÊNCIA ATIVA CONTEXTUAL ─────────────────────────────────────
function cbNormText(s){
  return String(s || '').toLowerCase().normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'');
}
function cbDaysBetween(dateStr, refStr){
  if (!dateStr) return 9999;
  const a = new Date(dateStr + 'T00:00:00');
  const b = new Date((refStr || todayStr()) + 'T00:00:00');
  return Math.round((b - a) / 86400000);
}
function taskText(t){ return cbNormText(`${t?.name||''} ${t?.dose||''} ${t?.obs||''}`); }
function isAnticoagulantOrAntiplatelet(t){
  const txt = taskText(t);
  return /(varfarina|marevan|warfarin|xarelto|rivaroxabana|eliquis|apixabana|pradaxa|dabigatrana|lixiana|edoxabana|heparina|enoxaparina|clexane|clopidogrel|plavix|aas|aspirina|acido acetilsalicilico|antiagregante|anticoagulante)/i.test(txt);
}
function getActiveMeds(){
  const today = todayStr();
  return (tasks || []).filter(t => t.type === 'med' && (t.repeat === 'daily' || t.date === today || !t.date));
}
function getRecentCareEvents(days){
  const limit = typeof days === 'number' ? days : 7;
  return (typeof getCareEvents === 'function' ? getCareEvents() : [])
    .filter(ev => cbDaysBetween(ev.date || todayStr()) >= 0 && cbDaysBetween(ev.date || todayStr()) <= limit);
}
function hasTodayTask(re){
  return (typeof getTodayTasks === 'function' ? getTodayTasks() : []).some(t => re.test(cbNormText(`${t.name||''} ${t.obs||''}`)));
}
function aiActionButton(action, label){
  if (!action) return '';
  return `<button class="ai-sug-action" onclick="${action}">${label || 'Abrir'}</button>`;
}

<script id="cb-wheel-router">
/* Desktop: rota wheel para o container scrollável sob o cursor.
   Home screen: grid com overflow:hidden — detecta painel por posição X do cursor. */
(function(){
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;

  function getScrollParent(el) {
    while (el && el !== document.documentElement) {
      var st = window.getComputedStyle(el);
      var oy = st.overflowY;
      if ((oy === 'auto' || oy === 'scroll') && el.scrollHeight > el.clientHeight) {
        return el;
      }
      el = el.parentElement;
    }
    return null;
  }

  document.addEventListener('wheel', function(e) {
    if (window.innerWidth < 768) return;

    // Tela Início: grid com overflow:hidden — getScrollParent falha.
    // Roteia pelo painel sob o cursor (esquerda=content, direita=right-panel).
    var home = document.getElementById('screen-home');
    if (home && home.classList.contains('active')) {
      var rightPanel = home.querySelector('.desktop-right-panel');
      var panel = (rightPanel && e.clientX >= rightPanel.getBoundingClientRect().left)
        ? rightPanel : home.querySelector('.content');
      if (panel && panel.scrollHeight > panel.clientHeight) {
        e.preventDefault();
        panel.scrollBy({ top: e.deltaY * (e.deltaMode === 1 ? 20 : 1), behavior: 'auto' });
      }
      return; // sempre consome o evento na home
    }

    // Outras telas: sobe a árvore até o scroll container natural
    var target = getScrollParent(e.target);
    if (!target) return;

    var atTop    = target.scrollTop <= 0;
    var atBottom = target.scrollTop + target.clientHeight >= target.scrollHeight - 1;
    if ((e.deltaY < 0 && atTop) || (e.deltaY > 0 && atBottom)) return;

    e.preventDefault();
    target.scrollBy({ top: e.deltaY * (e.deltaMode === 1 ? 20 : 1), behavior: 'auto' });
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

    flex-direction: column !important;
    justify-content: flex-start !important;
    align-items: stretch !important;
    padding: 20px 36px 16px !important;
    height: auto !important;
    min-height: 0 !important;
    gap: 0 !important;
  }

<script id="cb-wheel-router">
/* Desktop: rota wheel para o container scrollável sob o cursor.
   Home screen: grid com overflow:hidden — detecta painel por posição X do cursor. */
(function(){
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;

  function getScrollParent(el) {
    while (el && el !== document.documentElement) {
      var st = window.getComputedStyle(el);
      var oy = st.overflowY;
      if ((oy === 'auto' || oy === 'scroll') && el.scrollHeight > el.clientHeight) {
        return el;
      }
      el = el.parentElement;
    }
    return null;
  }

  document.addEventListener('wheel', function(e) {
    if (window.innerWidth < 768) return;

    // Tela Início: grid com overflow:hidden — getScrollParent falha.
    // Roteia pelo painel sob o cursor (esquerda=content, direita=right-panel).
    var home = document.getElementById('screen-home');
    if (home && home.classList.contains('active')) {
      var rightPanel = home.querySelector('.desktop-right-panel');
      var panel = (rightPanel && e.clientX >= rightPanel.getBoundingClientRect().left)
        ? rightPanel : home.querySelector('.content');
      if (panel && panel.scrollHeight > panel.clientHeight) {
        e.preventDefault();
        panel.scrollBy({ top: e.deltaY * (e.deltaMode === 1 ? 20 : 1), behavior: 'auto' });
      }
      return; // sempre consome o evento na home
    }

    // Outras telas: sobe a árvore até o scroll container natural
    var target = getScrollParent(e.target);
    if (!target) return;

    var atTop    = target.scrollTop <= 0;
    var atBottom = target.scrollTop + target.clientHeight >= target.scrollHeight - 1;
    if ((e.deltaY < 0 && atTop) || (e.deltaY > 0 && atBottom)) return;

    e.preventDefault();
    target.scrollBy({ top: e.deltaY * (e.deltaMode === 1 ? 20 : 1), behavior: 'auto' });
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

let deferredInstallPrompt = null;

// ── Detect iOS Safari ──
const isIOS = /iP(hone|od|ad)/.test(navigator.userAgent) && !window.MSStream;
const isSafari = /^((?!chrome|android).)*safari/i.test(navigator.userAgent);
const isInStandaloneMode = window.matchMedia('(display-mode: standalone)').matches || window.navigator.standalone;

window.addEventListener('beforeinstallprompt', (e) => {
  e.preventDefault();
  deferredInstallPrompt = e;
  // Show install banner after 3s if not already installed
  setTimeout(showInstallBanner, 3000);
});

function showInstallBanner() {
  if (isInStandaloneMode) return; // já instalado
  if (localStorage.getItem('pwa_install_dismissed')) return;
  if (isIOS && isSafari) {
    // iOS: não tem beforeinstallprompt, mostra banner manual
    const banner = document.getElementById('install-banner');
    if (banner) {
      banner.innerHTML = `
        <div class="install-banner-icon">📲</div>
        <div class="install-banner-text">
          <div class="install-banner-title">Instalar no iPhone/iPad</div>
          <div class="install-banner-sub">Toque em <b>compartilhar</b> → "Adicionar à Tela de Início"</div>
        </div>
        <button class="install-banner-close" onclick="dismissInstallBanner()">✕</button>
      `;
      banner.classList.add('show');
    }
    return;
  }
  if (!deferredInstallPrompt) return;
  const banner = document.getElementById('install-banner');
  if (banner) banner.classList.add('show');
}

function installPWA() {
  if (isIOS) {
    openInstallModal();
    return;
  }
  if (!deferredInstallPrompt) {
    openInstallModal();
    return;
  }
  deferredInstallPrompt.prompt();
  deferredInstallPrompt.userChoice.then(choice => {
    if (choice.outcome === 'accepted') {
      showToast('✅ CuidarBem instalado!');
    }
    deferredInstallPrompt = null;
    const banner = document.getElementById('install-banner');
    if (banner) banner.classList.remove('show');
  });
}

function openInstallModal() {
  const modal = document.getElementById('install-modal');
  if (modal) {
    // Esconde botão direto se não houver prompt disponível
    const btn = document.getElementById('install-direct-btn');
    if (btn) {
      btn.style.display = deferredInstallPrompt ? 'flex' : 'none';
    }
    modal.classList.add('open');
  }
}
function closeInstallModal() {
  const modal = document.getElementById('install-modal');
  if (modal) modal.classList.remove('open');
}

function installPWADirect() {
  if (!deferredInstallPrompt) {
    showToast('⚠️ Instalação automática não disponível. Siga as instruções abaixo.');
    return;
  }
  deferredInstallPrompt.prompt();
  deferredInstallPrompt.userChoice.then(choice => {
    if (choice.outcome === 'accepted') {
      showToast('✅ CuidarBem instalado!');
      closeInstallModal();
    }
    deferredInstallPrompt = null;
    const banner = document.getElementById('install-banner');
    if (banner) banner.classList.remove('show');
  });
}

  const ban

function loadCaregiverMode() {
  const stored = localStorage.getItem('cb_caregiver_mode');
  const isCare = stored !== '0'; // default: caregiver
  const btn = document.getElementById('caregiver-toggle');
  const desc = document.getElementById('mode-desc');
  if (btn) btn.classList.toggle('on', isCare);
  document.body.classList.toggle('patient-mode', !isCare);
  if (desc) desc.textContent = isCare ? 'Exibe todos os dados e controles' : 'Visão simplificada (paciente ativo)';
}

// ── 7. BP / GLUCOSE TRACKING ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
let currentVitalTab = 'bp';

// ── Classificação Pressão Arterial — SBC 2020 ────────────────────────────
function classifyBP(sys, dia) {
  if (sys < 90 || dia < 60)
    return { label:'Hipotensão', short:'Hipotensão', color:'#378add', bg:'#e6f1fb', emoji:'💙', level:-1 };
  if (sys < 120 && dia < 80)
    return { label:'Ótima', short:'Ótima', color:'#0f6e56', bg:'#e1f5ee', emoji:'💚', level:0 };
  if (sys <= 129 && dia <= 84)
    return { label:'Normal', short:'Normal', color:'#1d9e75', bg:'#e1f5ee', emoji:'✅', level:1 };
  if (sys <= 139 || dia <= 89)
    return { label:'Limítrofe', short:'Limítrofe', color:'#854f0b', bg:'#faeeda', emoji:'🟡', level:2 };
  if (sys <= 159 || dia <= 99)
    return { label:'HAS Estágio 1', short:'HAS 1', color:'#993c1d', bg:'#faece7', emoji:'🟠', level:3 };
  if (sys <= 179 || dia <= 109)
    return { label:'HAS Estágio 2', short:'HAS 2', color:'#a32d2d', bg:'#fcebeb', emoji:'🔴', level:4 };
  return { label:'HAS Estágio 3', short:'HAS 3', color:'#7b1f1f', bg:'#fcebeb', emoji:'🚨', level:5 };
}

// ── Classificação Glicemia — SBD / ADA 2024 ──────────────────────────────
function classifyGluc(val, moment) {
  if (val < 70)
    return { label:'Hipoglicemia', short:'Hipoglicemia', color:'#185fa5', bg:'#e6f1fb', emoji:'💙', level:-1 };
  const fasting = moment === 'jejum';
  const postMeal = ['pos-cafe','pos-almoco','pos-jantar'].incl
 das sub-telas */
  .header-sub {
    background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--green-600) 0%, #0f6e56 100%) !important;
    border-radius: 0 0 28px 28px !important;
    padding: 22px 38px !important;
    min-height: 88px !important;
    height: auto !important;
    display: flex !important;
    align-items: center !important;
    box-shadow: 0 8px 32px rgba(10,80,65,.18) !important;
  }
  .header-sub-title {
    font-size: 22px !important;
    font-weight: 800 !important;
    letter-spacing: -.03em !important;

}

  max-height: 0 !important;
  overflow: hidden !important;
  padding-top: 0 !important;

  height: auto !important;

}

<style id="cuidarbem-v75-9-scrollbar-global-por-guia">
/*
  Correção cirúrgica:
  - No mobile, quem rola é sempre a guia ativa (.screen.active), não o body.
  - Isso devolve a rolagem em Início, Relatórios, Saúde e Perfil.
  - Mantém a limitação contra “área fantasma” depois do último painel.
*/

  html,
  body{
    height: 100%;
    min-height: 100%;
    overflow: hidden !important;
    overscroll-behavior-y: none;

    height: 100svh;
  }

  .screen{
    display: block !important;
    position: absolute !important;
    inset: 0 0 auto 0 !important;
    width: 100% !important;
    height: 0 !important;
    min-height: 0 !important;
    max-height: 0 !important;
    overflow: hidden !important;
    visibility: hidden !important;
    pointer-events: none !important;
    padding-bottom: 0 !important;
  }

  .screen.active{
    position: relative !important;
    height: 100svh !important;
    min-height: 100svh !important;
    max-height: 100svh !important;
    overflow-y: auto !important;
    overflow-x: hidden !important;
    -webkit-overflow-scrolling: touch;
    overscroll-behavior-y: contain;
    visibility: visible !important;
    pointer-events: auto !important;
    padding-bottom: 0 !important;
    scrollbar-width: thin;
  }

  .screen.active::-webkit-scrollbar{
    width: 5px;
  }
  .screen.active::-webkit-scrollbar-track{
    background: transparent;

    border-radius: 999px;
  }

reen.active{
  padding-bottom: 0 !important;

<script id="cb-wheel-router">
/* Desktop: rota wheel para o container scrollável sob o cursor.
   Home screen: grid com overflow:hidden — detecta painel por posição X do cursor. */
(function(){
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;

  function getScrollParent(el) {
    while (el && el !== document.documentElement) {
      var st = window.getComputedStyle(el);
      var oy = st.overflowY;
      if ((oy === 'auto' || oy === 'scroll') && el.scrollHeight > el.clientHeight) {
        return el;
    
color:#94a3b8;line-height:1.6">
    Diagnóstico confirmou: efeitos GPU causavam os flashes.<br>
    Remoção permanente aplicada.
  </div>
  <button onclick="this.closest('#cb-diag-panel').classList.remove('open')">Fechar</button>

<script id="cb-wheel-router">
/* Desktop: rota wheel para o container scrollável sob o cursor.
   Home screen: grid com overflow:hidden — detecta painel por posição X do cursor. */
(function(){
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;

  function getScrollParent(el) {
    while (el && el !== document.documentElement) {
      var st = window.getComputedStyle(el);
      var oy = st.overflowY;
      if ((oy === 'auto' || oy === 'scroll') && el.scrollHeight > el.clientHeight) {
        return el;
      }
      el = el.parentElement;
    }
    return null;
  }

  document.addEventListener('wheel', function(e) {
    if (window.innerWidth < 768) return;

    // Tela Início: grid com overflow:hidden — getScrollParent falha.
    // Roteia pelo painel sob o cursor (esquerda=content, direita=right-panel).
    var home = document.getElementById('screen-home');
    if (home && home.classList.contains('active')) {
      var rightPanel = home.querySelector('.desktop-right-panel');
      var panel = (rightPanel && e.clientX >= rightPanel.getBoundingClientRect().left)
        ? rightPanel : home.querySelector('.content');
      if (panel && panel.scrollHeight > panel.clientHeight) {
        e.preventDefault();
        panel.scrollBy({ top: e.deltaY * (e.deltaMode === 1 ? 20 : 1), behavior: 'auto' });
      }
      return; // sempre consome o evento na home
    }

    // Outras telas: sobe a árvore até o scroll container natural
    var target = getScrollParent(e.target);
    if (!target) return;

    var atTop    = target.scrollTop <= 0;
    var atBottom = target.scrollTop + target.clientHeight >= target.scrollHeight - 1;
    if ((e.deltaY < 0 && atTop) || (e.deltaY > 0 && atBottom)) return;

    e.preventDefault();
    target.scrollBy({ top: e.deltaY * (e.deltaMode === 1 ? 20 : 1), behavior: 'auto' });
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>
